779 cards a CPU can't play

"Aim to Shine" Chika Takami (LSS/WE27-E26)
【AUTO】 [Put this card face down underneath a card named ""From Zero to One!" Riko Sakurauchi" in the middle position of your center stage as a marker] During your climax phase, when your other card named ""From Zero to One!" Riko Sakurauchi" becomes 【REST】, you may pay the cost. If you do, 【STAND】 that character.
【AUTO】 When your other character becomes 【REVERSE】 in battle, choose one of your red characters, and that character gets +1500 power until end of turn.
Needs doing by hand:
  - 【AUTO】 [Put this card face down underneath a ""From Zero to One!" Riko Sakurauchi" in the middle position of your center stage as a marker] During your climax phase, when your other ""From Zero to One!" Riko Sakurauchi" becomes 【REST】, you may pay the cost. If you do, 【STAND】 that character.
